## Module 08 — Approval and refinement portfolios

Each session executed exactly 300 safe-now packet checks, 300 candidate packet checks, and 300 CLEAN/FIX/REFINE checks. Safe-now records verified the committed bounded result and four-label allowlist. Candidate records independently recomputed each stored value digest. CLEAN/FIX/REFINE records checked exact proposal, fixture, and expected-outcome binding. Across x1 and x2 this produced 1,800 executed portfolio witnesses. Every task was local, reversible, synthetic, zero-spend, and free of external operations. Exact and blocked states were represented through the retained core outcomes and were not promoted merely to satisfy a numerical request.

## Module 09 — Retained negatives and recoveries

All 1,200 preregistered malformed subjects executed and were rejected: unknown proposal identifiers, duplicate vertex declarations, empty hyperedges, and outcome drift or promotion. Every rejection remains a failed witness and effective negative with zero completion credit. `NS7037-INTAKE-CATFILE-01` retained the original operational failure at zero completion credit: The first PowerShell persistent git cat-file binary-protocol reader stalled without producing a result. Recovery: A single persistent Node spawnSync git cat-file --batch process consumed each exact header, body, and terminator and verified all 141 source objects with zero failures. The failure changed no source repository byte and remains part of Neris local negative accounting. `NS7037-WORKTREE-ADD-01` retained the original operational failure at zero completion credit: The shared-repository git worktree add operation stalled after creating only the intended branch pointer at the exact source final; no target directory or registry entry appeared. Recovery: A fresh independent no-checkout sparse D-drive clone was created from the exact remote source branch, then the Neris branch was created at the exact source final. The failure changed no source repository byte and remains part of Neris local negative accounting. `NS7037-INTAKE-PROJECTION-01` retained the original operational failure at zero completion credit: A read-only PowerShell source-metadata search used an invalid empty pipeline element and raised a parser error. Recovery: The exact source final inventory and metadata were read by direct literal Git-object paths with no broad projection and no repository mutation. The failure changed no source repository byte and remains part of Neris local negative accounting. `NS7037-PLANNING-VERIFY-01` retained the original operational failure at zero completion credit: The first PowerShell per-file planning-manifest verifier completed without an auditable scalar projection. Recovery: A bounded Python byte-domain verifier replaced the wrapper and reported every checked field explicitly. The failure changed no source repository byte and remains part of Neris local negative accounting. `NS7037-PLANNING-VERIFY-02` retained the original operational failure at zero completion credit: The first Python fallback encoded a literal backslash-zero sequence instead of the Git object header NUL byte and therefore falsely reported all nine Git blob identifiers as mismatches while SHA-256 and byte counts were exact. Recovery: The verifier constructed the NUL as bytes([0]); all nine planning entries then matched their exact SHA-256, Git blob SHA-1, and byte counts. The failure changed no source repository byte and remains part of Neris local negative accounting. `NS7037-PLANNING-COMMIT-01` retained the original operational failure at zero completion credit: The first planning commit was refused because the fresh independent clone had no repository-local Git author identity. Recovery: The established source-repository author name and email were copied into this clone's local Git configuration only, then the same staged planning content was retried once. The failure changed no source repository byte and remains part of Neris local negative accounting. After the immutable planning commit, 5 additional operational failures were retained in a separate zero-credit external overlay at `D:\GHC-Archives\phase-receipts\neris-solane\v703-v7\operational-overlay.json` with SHA-256 `e5ae7ca4f14ae51a014b80f0adf51bdc86ef2d86f4e954b2b7f30300a2cd3d7f`. Its delta is 5 methods, 5 witnesses, 5 failed witnesses, and 5 negatives, with zero passing-witness, gap, gate, or completion credit. The repository-sealed counts and this external overlay must never be flattened.

## Module 10 — Skills runners and file policy

Twenty owner-local skills were created under the phase tree with concise discriminating descriptions and protected-boundary instructions; ten belong to x1 and ten to x2. They were validated with the current skill-creator quick validator and used only as owner-local guidance. Ten family-current TXT runners were self-tested and actually invoked, five per session. No skill or runner was installed globally, no existing global skill was overwritten, no new package was installed, and no external tool spend occurred. The source-faithful ledger, D-first isolation, file allowlist, and 2,000-file ceiling remained active.

## Module 11 — Accounting and practices

Neris local accounting is 33 methods, 3348 witnesses, 2142 passing witnesses, 1206 failed witnesses, 1206 negatives, 15 open gaps, and 15 exact gates. After exactly one incoming fold, the successor baseline is 5356 methods, 209378 witnesses, 159380 passing witnesses, 49998 failed witnesses, 58832 negatives, 1854 open gaps, and 1955 exact gates. The 300 new outcomes are exactly 255 completed, 15 represented, 15 open_gap, and 15 exact_gate. No failure, gap, gate, or source limitation was erased.

The four phase-local practices were finite combinatorics, formal methods, records and provenance management, and accessible evidence reporting. Their role is methodological only: they confer no academic, professional, operational, archival, accessibility-complete, legal, cultural, or Maori authority. The one recommendation for the prospective successor is formal methods engineering, framed as a bounded practice lens rather than a qualification or employment claim.

## Module 12 — Prospective guarded route

This committed baton is `PREPARED_NOT_SENT`. It does not record a delivery, acknowledgement, recipient reread, recipient start, or recipient completion. Only after Neris v703-v7 has four direct single-parent commits, zero merges, a clean pushed exact final, typed 0/0 divergence, fresh four-way equality, exact manifest and content-seal replay, strict JSON parsing, privacy checks, and one successful metadata-only canonical may the current live user instruction, roster, authorization, usage, duplicate, pause, redirect, rename, privacy, evidence, safety, and acknowledgement gates be reread. The prospective exact-title task is `Merrin Vale` for v703-v8. Resolve active and archived uniqueness immediately before any send. Send at most once. Acknowledged delivery is not recipient completion; accepted, opaque, unresolved, or failed delivery stops resends unless the user provides a new explicit instruction. Do not create a replacement task, contact a standby record, delegate, fork, or use a substitute endpoint.

## Module 13 — Terminal gate
